ROOMSYNC THIRD-PARTY NOTICES
===========================
Prepared 2026-11-03 for the Windows desktop and Android native distributions.

Scope and provenance
--------------------
This notice identifies 76 exact registry package versions resolved by
cargo metadata --locked for x86_64-pc-windows-gnu (desktop) and
aarch64-linux-android (Android native), including build dependencies. Packages
for unrelated target platforms that are only present in Cargo.lock are not
included. Registry package license/copyright files are reproduced below.
Identical texts are stored once and referenced by their [Tnnn] identifiers;
distinct copyright notices are retained. Formatting of the Rust toolchain's
HTML copyright inventory has been converted to plain text, without dropping
its notice inventory. The Rust runtime inventory is intentionally broader
than the code used by these particular applications.

This file does not set or grant a license for RoomSync's own source code.
License alternatives remain those declared by each upstream package. The
selected registry target graphs contain no mandatory copyleft-only package;
unicode-ident additionally requires its reproduced Unicode-3.0 notice.
This is an attribution collection, not a guarantee of legal completeness or
an assertion that every listed build dependency is linked into every binary.
No separate compiler executable is distributed as part of RoomSync.

ZXing Java attribution is supplied separately in the Android APK as
ZXING-LICENSE.txt and ZXING-NOTICE.txt (or equivalently named license assets).
Android system libraries are platform dependencies, not bundled copies.

Locked input fingerprints
-------------------------
apps/desktop/Cargo.lock: SHA-256 17b6fbdc022d74ff05dd7e531a4dc70d0207bb16b17a277bda2842841470ed25
apps/android/native/Cargo.lock: SHA-256 25629f7f27be409d82be13c10f4e1ea0d0729134ae05d8909175b55353a58c84

Desktop fingerprint refreshed for first-party RoomSync version 0.1.6.
The registry dependency versions and upstream notice texts are unchanged.

-----------------
Source: https://www.mingw-w64.org/
Notices are copied from the extracted MinGW-w64 distribution's
usr/share/doc/mingw-w64-common/copyright. Included sections cover its CRT and
libraries plus the corresponding license definitions; unrelated compiler,
SDK-header-only, and build-tool license sections are not asserted to apply.
